- **nmi-webhooks**: Receive and verify NMI (Network Merchants) webhooks. Use when setting up NMI webhook handlers, debugging Webhook-Signature verification, or handling transaction events like transaction.sale.success, transaction.auth.success, transaction.refund.success, and transaction.void.success. Note: NMI does NOT use Standard Webhooks — the Webhook-Signature header is "t=&lt;nonce&gt;,s=&lt;sig&gt;" (comma-separated) where t is a NONCE (not a Unix timestamp), and the signature is HMAC-SHA256 over "&lt;nonce&gt;.&lt;raw_body&gt;", lowercase hex. ([hookdeck/webhook-skills](https://github.com/hookdeck/webhook-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/nmi-webhooks) · ★102)

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-chemical-shift-curation))
- **nmr-chemical-shift-database-construction**: Use when a ROI-based NMR metabolite identification tool needs reference chemical shift and J-coupling (SRRM) libraries, and you must derive them from HMDB records and organize them by biological sample type. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-chemical-shift-database-construction))
- **nmr-chemical-shift-drift-assessment**: Use when a series of 1D-NMR spectra (e.g. a Daphnia magna extract series) shows chemical-shift drift between spectra and you need to quantify misalignment against a user-selected reference within a chosen spectral region before and after segmental alignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-chemical-shift-drift-assessment))
- **nmr-chemical-shift-interval-matching**: Use when you have isolated one or more regions-of-interest (ROIs) from a proton NMR spectrum (defined as lower and upper chemical-shift bounds in ppm) and need to systematically generate a list of plausible metabolite assignments from a reference database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-chemical-shift-interval-matching) · 2 copies)
- **nmr-chemical-shift-library-searching**: Use when a query HSQC spectrum (list of 1H/13C shift pairs) must be identified or annotated by peak-shift matching against a reference library, or when reproducing the SMART 2.0 / NMRShiftDB baseline of a DeepSAT benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-chemical-shift-library-searching))
- **nmr-chemical-shift-ph-dependence-modeling**: Use when simulating 1D or 2D metabolomic 1H NMR spectra at a target pH different from the standard pH0 (usually 7.4) and you must reposition peaks that shift with pH. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-chemical-shift-ph-dependence-modeling))
- **nmr-chemical-shift-prediction**: Use when you need predicted 1H NMR shifts across a compound structure library (e.g., SMILES/SDF from the Natural Products Atlas) to assess dereplication coverage or classify shielded vs. deshielded resonances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-chemical-shift-prediction))
- **nmr-compound-inference**: Analyze 1D 1H or 13C NMR data with nmrglue, generate spectrum plots and peak tables, compare against standard NMR libraries such as BMRB, HMDB, SDBS, and nmrshiftdb2, then produce a ranked candidate-compound report with evidence, caveats, and direct links. ([synthetic-sciences/openscience](https://github.com/synthetic-sciences/openscience/tree/HEAD/backend/cli/skills/chemistry/nmr-compound-inference) · ★4.4k · 3 copies)
- **nmr-concentration-calibration**: Use when a series of 1D NMR spectra (individual, pseudo-2D, or tabulated text files) has been fitted and fitted signal areas must be converted to mM concentrations and time courses with uncertainties. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-concentration-calibration))
- **nmr-coupling-constant-calculation**: Use when a one-dimensional 1H NMR spectrum (JCAMP-DX) has been loaded and peak-picked, and per-peak coupling constants and multiplicity labels must be derived automatically from the splitting pattern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-coupling-constant-calculation))
- **nmr-cross-peak-overlap-resolution**: Use when a processed 2D or pseudo-3D NMR spectrum contains significantly overlapped cross-peaks that prevent straightforward peak picking, and quantitatively analyzable individual components (positions, line shapes, intensities) are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-cross-peak-overlap-resolution))
- **nmr-data-format-parsing**: Use when raw 1D 1H NMR spectra in Bruker IVDr format (600 MHz) must be loaded and made ppm-addressable before spectral calibration to the glucose anomeric proton at 5.233 ppm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-data-format-parsing))
- **nmr-dataset-retrieval-from-metabolomics-repositories**: Use when a task requires acquiring public NMR metabolomics data sets from MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench, or when raw repository file-system formats must be converted into analysis-ready formats for MetaboAnalyst or Workflows4Metabolomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-dataset-retrieval-from-metabolomics-repositories))
- **nmr-doublet-signal-interpretation**: Use when 1H NMR plasma spectra (e.g. FGENTCARD metabo_SE SummarizedExperiment) contain unassigned peaks linked to a phenotype and you need a metabolite assignment via driver-signal correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-doublet-signal-interpretation))
- **nmr-feature-differential-analysis**: Use when you have a speaq 2.0 peak-picked/grouped feature matrix (samples x features) plus class metadata for 1D NMR spectra, and you need to find features that significantly differ between two classes (e.g. red vs white wines). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-feature-differential-analysis))
- **nmr-feature-matrix-construction**: Use when raw 1D NMR spectra must be reduced to a feature matrix of peak groups for downstream statistics (PCA, differential analysis), and binning would lose information. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-feature-matrix-construction))
- **nmr-fid-batch-processing**: Use when comparing processing speed of NMR software on a batch of 1D 1H-NMR FIDs requiring identical parameters (e.g., zero-filling to 131072 points, 0.3 Hz exponential line broadening, FT, automated phase correction), or when scripting batch NMR pre-processing in Python. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-fid-batch-processing))
- **nmr-fid-group-delay-correction**: Use when raw Bruker FIDs (time-domain, complex) carry a DSP digital-filter group delay recorded in the acqus parameters and you need phase-corrected FIDs prior to apodization, zero filling, and Fourier transform. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-fid-group-delay-correction))
- **nmr-fid-visualization**: Use when raw Bruker 'fid' files have just been extracted into a pandas DataFrame (one row per sample) and you need a sanity check that FID traces loaded correctly before group delay removal or Fourier transform. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-fid-visualization))
- **nmr-fit-uncertainty-estimation**: Use when 1D or pseudo-2D NMR spectra contain overlapping multiplet peaks (e.g. a triplet plus a quadruplet) or low signal-to-noise, and you need quantitative parameters with reported uncertainty rather than simple integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-fit-uncertainty-estimation))
- **nmr-fourier-transform-processing**: Use when raw Bruker time-domain 2D NMR FID data (`fid` or `ser`) must be converted to a phased 2D spectrum via the automated chain: zero-filling, apodization, water suppression, Fourier transformation, phase correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-fourier-transform-processing))
- **nmr-gradient-decay-curve-fitting**: Use when the input is a pseudo-3D NMR series (e.g., pulsed-field-gradient DOSY, or T1/T2/titration/kinetics series of 2D planes) and per-component parameters such as diffusion coefficient D must be extracted, especially when peaks overlap. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-gradient-decay-curve-fitting))
- **nmr-hsqc-spectral-library-matching**: Use when you have HSQC mixture spectra (grid spectra or peak lists) and a compound spectral library (e.g. 501-metabolite MetaboMiner library), and need to detect constituent compounds and estimate concentrations without peak-picking the mixture. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-hsqc-spectral-library-matching))
- **nmr-inadequate-spectra-acquisition-and-preprocessing**: Use when when you have raw INADEQUATE NMR spectral data (e.g., from a query sample or library) and need to extract peak coordinates and intensities as a first step toward identifying metabolite peak networks or comparing against a metabolite database. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-inadequate-spectra-acquisition-and-preprocessing))
- **nmr-input-vector-preparation-for-cnn-transformer**: Use when raw 1H NMR spectra (ppm, intensity pairs) and/or 13C chemical-shift lists must be turned into the fixed-length concatenated input vectors expected by the NMR2Struct spectrum-to-structure model before training or single-spectrum inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-input-vector-preparation-for-cnn-transformer))
- **nmr-ir-ms-spectra-type-mapping**: Use when a batch of spectra files (JCAMP-DX, mzML, Bruker, RAW, FID, BagIt or ZIP) must be automatically classified as NMR, IR, MS or unknown before further conversion or rendering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-ir-ms-spectra-type-mapping))
- **nmr-jcamp-dx-block-parsing**: Use when a 1H or 13C NMR input file is in JCAMP-DX (.jdx) format and the spectrum must be loaded block-by-block before peak detection or multiplicity analysis, as done in ChemSpectra's NMR workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-jcamp-dx-block-parsing))
- **nmr-jres-tissue-distribution-quantification**: Use when quantifying how metabolites derived from a 13C-labeled amino acid diet are distributed across multiple tissues from integrated 13C-JRES spectra, to assign each compound an enrichment pattern (liver-enriched, liver-depleted, co-enriched). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-jres-tissue-distribution-quantification))
- **nmr-kinetics-deconvolution**: Separate spectral mixture estimation from kinetic-model fitting. Invoke for: extracting reaction time courses from overlapping NMR spectra; not for fitting a rate law to unverified peak areas. ([Aneskw/chem-agent-toolkit](https://github.com/Aneskw/chem-agent-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/nmr-kinetics-deconvolution) · ★2)
- **nmr-library-component-matching**: Use when a mixture 1H NMR spectrum must be deconvolved against reference component spectra and peaks overlap almost completely, lineshapes are distorted, or peaks are shifted — e.g., low-field benchtop (Magritek 43 MHz) data where peak-picking tools fail. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-library-component-matching))
- **nmr-match-ratio-scoring**: Use when candidate metabolites have been matched to one or more user-defined NMR spectral ROIs and you must filter out metabolites whose SRRMs mostly lie outside the analyzed regions before categorization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-match-ratio-scoring))
- **nmr-metabolite-assignment**: Use when 1H-NMR spectra (e.g. plasma metabonomic profiles) yield unassigned peaks linked to a phenotype and you need metabolite-level assignment via co-correlation of ppm signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolite-assignment))
- **nmr-metabolite-distribution-visualization**: Use when a CSV/TSV of Nightingale 1H-NMR metabolite concentrations (with optional phenotype columns) needs initial inspection: distribution checks, missingness overview, and comparison against the BBMRI-nl reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolite-distribution-visualization))
- **nmr-metabolite-identification**: Use when raw 1H-NMR spectra from cell extracts (e.g., hESC-derived neural stages acquired on a Bruker Avance 800 MHz with cryoprobe) must be converted into a per-sample list of identified metabolites for quantification or pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolite-identification))
- **nmr-metabolite-identity-confirmation**: Use when you have preprocessed 1H NMR spectral data with an unknown or ambiguous peak (e.g., at a specific chemical shift δ), and you need to determine its chemical identity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-metabolite-identity-confirmation))
- **nmr-metabolite-integral-pca**: Use when 1D ¹H NMR plasma/serum spectra have already been processed with SMolESY-select into a metabolite-integral matrix (22 metabolites × N samples), and the goal is exploratory biomarker discovery via dimension reduction rather than fitting/deconvolution-based profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolite-integral-pca))
- **nmr-metabolite-phenotype-correlation-computation**: Use when a matrix of projected metabolic scores (e.g., mortality, MetaboAge, T2D, COVID-severity surrogates) must be correlated with metabolites or phenotypes, or when event prediction over time (Kaplan-Meier) is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolite-phenotype-correlation-computation))
- **nmr-metabolite-profiling**: Profile metabolites from NMR spectra with a reproducible workflow. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/router_collection/workflows/nmr-metabolite-profiling) · ★16)
- **nmr-metabolite-trend-categorization**: Use when candidate metabolites from ROIs in 1H NMR spectra (e.g. a 0.5-4.5 ppm serum region) must be filtered by match ratio, significance, HMDB concentration, and disease relevance, then sorted into an output table for a disease-vs-control comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolite-trend-categorization))
- **nmr-metabolomic-quality-control-reporting**: Use when you have uploaded a pre-analytical data table containing sample metadata, processing delay timestamps (pre- and post-centrifugation), and NMR metabolomic measurements for a cohort of plasma or serum samples, and you need to assess how processing delays affect metabolite concentrations and. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-metabolomic-quality-control-reporting) · 3 copies)
- **nmr-metabolomics-biomarker-annotation**: Use when many mutually correlated candidate biomarkers (e.g. 226 NMR metabolic measures) must be pruned to a subset independently associated with a survival endpoint, using repeated meta-analytic forward-backward selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolomics-biomarker-annotation))
- **nmr-metabolomics-class-discrimination-roc-analysis**: Use when re-analyzing a public NMR metabolomics study (MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench study id) to reproduce reported binary class-discrimination AUCs, e.g. disease vs control groups with labels such as bacterial, viral, control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolomics-class-discrimination-roc-analysis))
- **nmr-metabolomics-data-refitting-from-deposited-tables**: Use when deposited delay-experiment data tables must be re-analyzed to reproduce or extend metabolite stability time points (20% change threshold) for EDTA plasma, LiHep plasma, or serum from pre-/postcentrifugation delays. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolomics-data-refitting-from-deposited-tables))
- **nmr-metabolomics-feature-processing**: Use when input is a raw NMR metabolomics table with sample IDs and metabolite columns containing missing values, and you need an imputed, label-annotated matrix for disease-prediction fine-tuning or AUC evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolomics-feature-processing))
- **nmr-metabolomics-phenotype-association-count-extraction**: Use when the goal is to quantify how many 1H-NMR metabolomics features associate with a clinical endpoint and tabulate surrogate-model performance (AUC, LOBOV, calibration deltas) for a cohort such as LLS_PAROFFs against a reference like BBMRI-nl. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolomics-phenotype-association-count-extraction))
- **nmr-metabolomics-profiling-workflow**: Use when you have NMR metabolomics data (1D/2D spectra or FIDs) and want a quantified, identified metabolite profile — spectral preprocessing (phase/baseline/referencing, binning), metabolite identification by chemical shift, quantification, and group statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/workflows/nmr-metabolomics-profiling) · 2 copies)
- **nmr-metabolomics-surrogate-score-calibration**: Use when projected 1H-NMR surrogate scores (e.g. 'high hscrp') from MiMIR need recalibration to a desired cohort, and calibration gain must be quantified on a held-out fraction of samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolomics-surrogate-score-calibration))
- **nmr-metabolomics-surrogate-validation**: Use when projected surrogate scores (e.g. diabetes, mortality, MetaboAge-adjacent clinical surrogates) from Nightingale Health 1H-NMR metabolomics must be quality-checked against available phenotypes and published LOBOV AUC references. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolomics-surrogate-validation))
- **nmr-metabolomics-table-verification**: Reference for validating PyINETA INADEQUATE network results against a published table: compare constructed-network, matched-network, and compound counts to the paper's reported values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolomics-table-verification))
- **nmr-metabolomics-validation-cohort-analysis**: Use when NMR metabolomics samples have known pre- or postcentrifugation delays and you need to test whether delay-prediction models (fit on a training cohort) robustly predict concentrations in an independent validation cohort, with a 10% MPE accuracy threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabolomics-validation-cohort-analysis))
- **nmr-metabonomic-dataset-loading**: Use when starting an MWASTools-based metabonomic analysis or vignette-style reproducibility task that needs a ready-made plasma 1H NMR dataset with annotated QC samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-metabonomic-dataset-loading))
- **nmr-mixture-deconvolution-penalty-tuning**: Use when a 1H NMR mixture spectrum must be quantified against library component spectra despite overlapping peaks, unexpected shifts (e.g. different solvents or temperature), low-field benchtop acquisition, or distorted lineshapes from poor shimming. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-mixture-deconvolution-penalty-tuning))
- **nmr-mixture-dereplication-via-network-matching**: Use when you have TOCSY and HSQC spectra for a sample set of microbial extracts/prefractions and need to dereplicate known compound families (e.g., novobiocin in prefractions 1565C/1565D) or group samples into regions of related chemical space. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-mixture-dereplication-via-network-matching))
- **nmr-mixture-quantification**: Use when 1H NMR mixture spectra contain overlapping peaks, shifted resonances (e.g., 36.7 °C sample vs 22.27 °C library), lineshape distortions from inhomogeneous fields, or low-field benchtop resolution, and relative molar proportions of components are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-mixture-quantification))
- **nmr-mixture-spectra-cnn-training**: Use when the goal is compound identification in NMR spectra of mixtures and you have (or can generate) superposed, noise-augmented NMR spectral pairs to train a CNN classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-mixture-spectra-cnn-training))
- **nmr-modality-ablation-analysis**: Use when you have a trained multitask machine learning model for structure prediction, test set molecules with paired ¹H and ¹³C NMR spectra, and need to understand the marginal contribution of each NMR modality or justify multimodal input design. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/nmr-modality-ablation-analysis) · 2 copies)
- **nmr-moving-average-smoothing-filter**: Use when processing 1D 1H NMR serum/plasma spectra (e.g. Bruker IVDr 600 MHz acquisitions) where targeted low-concentration metabolite spin systems are obscured by noise and need a standardized 11-point moving-average denoising before pattern-based assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-moving-average-smoothing-filter))
- **nmr-multiplicity-assignment-from-splitting-pattern**: Use when processing 1D 1H NMR spectra (e.g. JCAMP-DX from ChemSpectra/Chemotion ELN) and you need automatic peak picking plus coupling constant calculation and multiplicity assignment for the detected signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-multiplicity-assignment-from-splitting-pattern))
- **nmr-network-visualization-and-interpretation**: Use when you have 2D NMR spectra (heteronuclear: HSQC, HMBC; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-network-visualization-and-interpretation))
- **nmr-outlier-plate-detection**: Use when processing UK Biobank NMR biomarker data after regression-based technical-covariate adjustment, when a small number of 96-well shipment plates show unexplained plate-level shifts across a swathe of biomarkers and their samples should be set to missing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-outlier-plate-detection))
- **nmr-parameter-sensitivity-analysis**: Use when an mcfNMR run has produced F1 scores over scanned (r, theta) combinations for multiple MCF setups and you need to determine parameter robustness and optimal (r, theta) settings per setup. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-parameter-sensitivity-analysis))
- **nmr-peak-deconvolution**: Use when when you have raw or processed 1D NMR spectra (FID or frequency-domain format) and need to extract individual peak identities and quantitative parameters in tabular form. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-peak-deconvolution))
- **nmr-peak-json-serialization**: Use when you have proton (1H) and carbon-13 (13C) NMR peak measurements from a molecular sample and need to classify the molecule using the SMART 3 deep learning API. The peaks must be reformatted from their native instrument output into JSON before submission to the /api/smart3/search endpoint. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-peak-json-serialization))
- **nmr-peak-list-based-mcf-variant-execution**: Use when the target spectrum is available as a pre-picked peak list (csv with 1H, 13C, optional weights) and you want compound identification that is robust to matrix-induced peak shifts, e.g. pH-disturbed plasma samples like N907. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-peak-list-based-mcf-variant-execution))
- **nmr-peak-network-matching**: Use when you have filtered peak networks from an INADEQUATE NMR spectrum and need to identify metabolites by matching each network to a simulated INADEQUATE database using CSMT, NCMT, and DQMT thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-peak-network-matching))
- **nmr-peak-pairing-between-spectra**: Use when experimental mixture NMR spectra and pure-component reference spectra are both available and you need to measure how far the same compound's peaks shift between mixtures, e.g. to derive or validate a chemical shift tolerance interval in ppm. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-peak-pairing-between-spectra))
- **nmr-peak-picking**: Use when a 1D 1H/13C NMR spectrum (JCAMP-DX, or Bruker/FID after conversion) must be reduced to a machine-readable list of detected signal positions and intensities under a default or user-defined threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-peak-picking))
- **nmr-peak-picking-and-multiplicity-assignment**: Use when a series of 1D or pseudo-2D NMR spectra (1H, 13C, 31P, 15N) must be quantified and each signal of interest needs peak positions and multiplicity defined before multiplet-model fitting, especially with overlapping peaks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-peak-picking-and-multiplicity-assignment))
- **nmr-peak-prediction-via-database-lookup**: Use when you have extracted peak data (chemical shift values in ppm, multiplicities, integration) from a processed NMR spectrum (JCAMP, RAW, or mzML format) and need to match these peaks against known NMR signals to propose or confirm structural assignments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-peak-prediction-via-database-lookup))
- **nmr-peak-quality-filtering**: Use when you have a CSV or table-formatted input spectral peak list (chemical shifts, intensities, metadata) destined for NMRformer metabolite identification, and you observe or suspect that the list contains noisy peaks or peaks with chemical shifts that do not match known reference standards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-peak-quality-filtering))
- **nmr-peak-regrouping-with-ppm-distance**: Use when peak grouping of 1D NMR spectra (e.g., the wine dataset processed with speaq 2.0) has produced suspect groups, indicated by a low mean silhouette value despite a high average peak SNR. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-peak-regrouping-with-ppm-distance))
- **nmr-peak-table-generation**: Use when you have raw 1D NMR spectra (in NMRPipe or similar format) from biological samples (urine, worm, or other metabolomics experiments) and need to extract a structured peak table with chemical shifts and intensities for downstream metabolite identification or quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-peak-table-generation))
- **nmr-phase-angle-statistics-visualization**: Use when comparing automatic phase correction algorithms (e.g. MetaboLabPy baseline-based, MetaboLab apk5, TopSpin apk0.noe, MNova) on replicate NMR series and you need a quantitative within-series phase-consistency benchmark plus comparison figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-phase-angle-statistics-visualization))
- **nmr-plasma-biomarker-extraction**: Use when a relative-concentration matrix (e.g. 40 MDS family plasma samples x 38 metabolites from NMRQNet) needs group-difference biomarker calls between patient and control groups via PCA, OPLS-DA, and t-tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-plasma-biomarker-extraction))
- **nmr-preprocessing-pipeline-interfacing**: Use when raw Bruker 1H-NMR metabolomics data (acqus parameters + FIDs, e.g. a Zenodo NMRDataset zip) must be converted into a sample x bin intensity table for multivariate analysis such as PCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-preprocessing-pipeline-interfacing))
- **nmr-processing-time-benchmarking**: Use when you need to compare wall-clock processing speed of NMR software (MetaboLabPy, MNova, TopSpin, MetaboLab scripting) on a defined batch of 1D 1H-NMR spectra with fixed processing parameters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-processing-time-benchmarking))
- **nmr-profiling**: Profile metabolites from NMR spectra using resonance patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skill-collections/tree/HEAD/tests/fixtures/router_collection/leaves/nmr-profiling) · ★16)
- **nmr-pulse-sequence-signal-modeling**: Use when building or validating forward signal models for 1D/2D NMR relaxation or diffusion experiments (T1/T2 relaxation, PFG diffusion) prior to multi-exponential fitting or regularized numerical inversion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-pulse-sequence-signal-modeling))
- **nmr-quantification-accuracy-comparison**: Use when simulated 1D NMR spectra with known ground-truth peak parameters are available and you need to quantify how a perturbation (e.g., zero-order phase distortion) or preprocessing choice degrades peak parameter estimation relative to a phased baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-quantification-accuracy-comparison))
- **nmr-raw-data-availability-assessment**: Use when you need to quantify how many publicly available studies in a metabolomics repository (e.g. Metabolomics Workbench) use NMR, involve Homo sapiens, or expose raw vendor acquisition files, broken down by sample type or instrument vendor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-raw-data-availability-assessment))
- **nmr-raw-spectral-file-parsing**: Use when raw NMR-based metabolomics files (acqu/acqus, fid, 1r) from MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench studies must be reconstructed into a sample-by-spectral-point matrix and binned to exactly 1000 columns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-raw-spectral-file-parsing))
- **nmr-reference-spectrum-retrieval**: Use when you need reference-like 1D 1H NMR spectra for blood/urine/CSF and no suitable experimental spectra are available, or when benchmarking spectral algorithms against simulated-vs-real comparisons. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-reference-spectrum-retrieval))
- **nmr-reference-spectrum-retrieval-from-hmdb**: Use when building simulated 1H NMR case-vs-control spectra from known metabolites, or when detected peak ppm values must be queried against a reference metabolite database such as HMDB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-reference-spectrum-retrieval-from-hmdb))
- **nmr-referencing-calibration**: Use when a raw 1D 1H NMR spectrum of serum/plasma (e.g. Bruker IVDr 600 MHz format) must be ppm-calibrated before metabolite assignment, integration, or cross-sample comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-referencing-calibration))
- **nmr-region-of-interest-metabolite-identification**: Use when you have 1D proton NMR spectra of biological samples (serum, saliva, urine, CSF, sweat, tissues) with pre-determined ROIs, trends, and significance levels, and need to identify which HMDB metabolites explain those ROI patterns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-region-of-interest-metabolite-identification))
- **nmr-signal-calibration-glucose-anomeric**: Use when 1D 1H NMR spectra of serum or plasma are about to enter a spin-system assignment workflow and must first be referenced to a common chemical-shift axis, e.g. before running SMolESY-select or comparing spectral windows across samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-signal-calibration-glucose-anomeric))
- **nmr-signal-substructure-correlation**: Use when interpreting a trained DeepSAT HSQC model: you need to verify that specific HSQC peaks drive predicted chemical fingerprints or structure classes by masking spectral regions and measuring attribution changes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-signal-substructure-correlation))
- **nmr-simulated-spectra-generation**: Use when you need controlled benchmark spectra with known ground truth — e.g., to validate SAND quantification (~0.9 correlation), cluster recovery (~74%), or robustness to 0.2 rad phase distortion. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-simulated-spectra-generation))
- **nmr-solvent-residual-signal-removal**: Use when you hold a pandas DataFrame of FIDs (grpd_df) that has already been through group-delay correction, and dominant residual solvent decays must be removed before apodization and Fourier transform in a 1H-NMR metabolomics pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-solvent-residual-signal-removal))
- **nmr-solvent-suppression**: Use when processing raw Bruker 2D FID data (fid/ser) from aqueous samples where the dominant solvent peak must be suppressed automatically before Fourier transformation and phase correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-solvent-suppression))
- **nmr-spectra-compound-identification**: Use when you need to identify known compounds in flavor/food mixture samples from 1H NMR spectra rather than run full spectral deconvolution, or when you want to test whether a trained classifier generalizes to unseen compounds by retraining on a reduced standard set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-compound-identification))
- **nmr-spectra-dataframe-processing**: Use when Fourier-transformed 1H-NMR spectra are held as a pandas DataFrame (samples × ppm) and need per-spectrum phase correction or further Protomix preprocessing before statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-dataframe-processing))
- **nmr-spectra-deep-learning-encoding**: Use when when you have preprocessed 1H NMR spectral data from flavor mixtures or similar compound identification tasks, and you need to identify which compounds are present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectra-deep-learning-encoding))
- **nmr-spectra-file-import**: Use when raw 1D 1H/13C NMR FID data from Bruker or Agilent/Varian instruments (Bruker folder structure with 1r files), or spectra in nmrML format, must be loaded into NMRProcFlow before baseline correction, alignment, or binning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-file-import))
- **nmr-spectra-format-conversion**: Use when raw NMR metabolomics data pulled from MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench must be reshaped from repository-specific layouts (.fid, .mzXML, .CNX, .nmrML, .TABULAR, Bruker directories) into an analysis-ready standard format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-format-conversion))
- **nmr-spectra-grouping-correction**: Use when peak-picked, already-grouped NMR spectra contain suspicious peak groups — e.g., a group with a low mean silhouette value (~0.25) but a high average SNR — and the groups must be reassigned before building the feature matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-grouping-correction))
- **nmr-spectra-integral-binning**: Use when a preprocessed, window-selected NMR spectra matrix (spectra_df, samples x chemical shift points) is still too high-dimensional for multivariate statistics and you need a bucketed feature table with one integrated intensity per fixed ppm bin, while coarsely tolerating residual peak shifts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-integral-binning))
- **nmr-spectra-intelligent-binning**: Use when bucketing aligned, baseline-corrected 1D 1H NMR spectra into a sample x bucket intensity matrix and peak-shift artifacts or information loss from equally sized bins are a concern. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-intelligent-binning))
- **nmr-spectra-ppm-shifting**: Use when raw 1D 1H NMR spectra of serum/plasma (e.g. 600 MHz Bruker IVDr) show uncalibrated chemical-shift axes and a glucose-referenced, reproducible ppm scale is required before metabolite assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-ppm-shifting))
- **nmr-spectra-preprocessing**: Use when when you have raw 1D NMR spectroscopic data (urine, worm, or other biological samples) in a format supported by NMRBox/NMRPipe and need to generate peak tables with automatic phasing, baseline correction, and spectral deconvolution without manual intervention. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectra-preprocessing))
- **nmr-spectra-preprocessing**: Use when a series of raw 1D-NMR metabolomics spectra must be transformed into an aligned, scaled, variance-stabilised matrix ready for multivariate statistical analysis or metabolic tracer data analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-preprocessing))
- **nmr-spectra-preprocessing-and-feature-extraction**: Use when when you have raw or lightly processed 1D NMR spectra (¹H and/or ¹³C) from unknown organic compounds and need to extract latent spectral features prior to structure elucidation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/nmr-spectra-preprocessing-and-feature-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **nmr-spectra-referencing-shifting**: Reference for aligning chemical shifts of raw INADEQUATE spectra (via basic shifting) before PyINETA peak picking, when input spectra have not yet been referenced and picked_peaks.csv coordinates must fall on the correct chemical-shift axis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-referencing-shifting))
- **nmr-spectra-series-shift-export**: Use when a series of 1D-NMR spectra shows chemical-shift variability that must be corrected before multivariate statistical analysis, and per-spectrum optimum shifts need to be computed and exported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-series-shift-export))
- **nmr-spectra-viewer-grouping**: Use when you have a set of 1D 1H or 13C NMR spectra plus an experimental-design table recording factor levels per spectrum, and you need spectra rendered overlaid or stacked, colored by factor level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-viewer-grouping))
- **nmr-spectra-visual-inspection**: Use when raw 1D NMR spectra (FID from Bruker or Agilent/Varian) need to be visually explored — e.g. to locate a reference resonance for ppm calibration, check alignment, or compare spectra grouped by experimental factor. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-visual-inspection))
- **nmr-spectra-wavelet-peak-detection**: Use when raw 1D NMR spectra (e.g. 40 wine spectra × 8712 ppm points, or 31 urine spectra) must be reduced to a feature matrix of peaks, avoiding binning's information loss and user-dependent bin boundaries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-wavelet-peak-detection))
- **nmr-spectra-webgl-contour-visualization**: Use when an already-processed 2D or pseudo-3D NMR spectrum needs interactive contour or 3D surface-mesh rendering that is architecture- and OS-independent, with no installation required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectra-webgl-contour-visualization))
- **nmr-spectral-adaptive-binning**: Use when a 1D frequency-domain (ppm-axis) metabolomics NMR spectrum must be partitioned into frequency bins and converted into per-bin time-domain FIDs for time-domain deconvolution or peak-table generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-adaptive-binning))
- **nmr-spectral-assignment**: Use when analyzing 1D 1H NMR spectra of serum/plasma samples (e.g. IVDr 600 MHz acquisition) to assign and quantify specific metabolite spin systems via spectral-window binning rather than full spectral deconvolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-assignment))
- **nmr-spectral-binning-and-normalization-chaining**: Use when you have phase-corrected, baseline-corrected, peak-aligned 1H-NMR spectra (e.g. Protomix spectra_df from Bruker FID processing) and need a binned, normalized sample-by-bin matrix as input to PCA or similar statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-binning-and-normalization-chaining))
- **nmr-spectral-comparative-analysis**: Use when you have 2D NMR spectral data (heteronuclear and/or homonuclear experiments) from multiple samples in a natural products library or mixture, and you need to identify common structural scaffolds, correlate features across samples, or prioritize samples for further structural annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectral-comparative-analysis))
- **nmr-spectral-data-augmentation**: Use when you need to construct an augmented training/validation/test set of NMR spectral pairs from a small flavor-standard spectral database, or need pseudo-mixture spectra (e.g., augmented F10) to benchmark a component-identification model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-data-augmentation--1736e19d29ffebdd))
- **nmr-spectral-data-augmentation**: Use when training deep-learning metabolite identification/quantification models (e.g. CRNN in NMRQNet) but no public annotated NMR spectra database exists and training spectra must be synthesized from a metabolite reference spectral library. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-data-augmentation--b8486544790302bd))
- **nmr-spectral-data-format-serialization**: Use when you have generated 1D FID time-domain data and Fourier-transformed frequency-domain 1H NMR spectra, or computed 2D COSY/HSQC correlation matrices, and need to write them to disk in a format that standard NMR software (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectral-data-format-serialization))
- **nmr-spectral-data-io**: Use when input is raw or already processed 1D ¹H NMR serum/plasma spectra (e.g. Bruker IVDr 600 MHz acquisitions from MetaboLights MTBLS395) that must be read into memory, normalized to a common chemical-shift reference, or smoothed to recover low-abundance metabolite signals. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-data-io))
- **nmr-spectral-deconvolution**: Use when 1D NMR spectra (e.g., urine metabolomics FIDs) have heavily overlapping peaks that bin-based integration cannot resolve, and the goal is an automated peak table for quantification, spike-in linearity checks, or correlation-network annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-deconvolution))
- **nmr-spectral-encoder-feature-concatenation**: Use when building or reproducing an end-to-end spectrum-to-structure model whose input is concatenated 1H/13C NMR spectra and whose downstream head is a pretrained transformer generating SMILES. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-encoder-feature-concatenation))
- **nmr-spectral-feature-extraction**: Use when when you have raw or preprocessed 1H NMR spectral tensors from flavor or chemical mixtures and need to generate high-level feature representations that capture both fine-grained local patterns (e.g., peak multiplet structure, coupling constants) and global spectral context (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectral-feature-extraction))
- **nmr-spectral-global-shift-correction**: Use when a CRNN reconstruction of a 1H NMR plasma spectrum is misaligned relative to the target spectrum and metabolite candidates must be aligned before local quantification optimization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-global-shift-correction))
- **nmr-spectral-inference**: Use when given 1H NMR spectral data of a flavor mixture and a standards library, and the goal is to predict the mixture's component list and quantify identification accuracy (ACC/TPR/FPR). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-inference))
- **nmr-spectral-noise-augmentation**: Use when training a supervised NMR mixture-identification model and labeled experimental mixture spectra are too scarce — synthesize realistic mixtures from measured pure-compound 1H NMR spectra with variability simulation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-noise-augmentation))
- **nmr-spectral-noise-region-selection**: Use when a raw 1D NMR spectrum (ppm scale plus intensity array) has a distorted baseline and you need baseline points selected only in signal-free regions before spline fitting and subtraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-noise-region-selection))
- **nmr-spectral-peak-coordinate-matching**: Use when a target NMR mixture spectrum and a library of compound spectra are both available as coordinate/weight tables (csv with '1H', '13C', optional 'weights' columns) and the goal is compound identification plus concentration estimation by optimal spectral fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-peak-coordinate-matching))
- **nmr-spectral-peak-detection**: Use when the input is a simulated (e.g. MetAssimulo 2.0) or real 1D 1H, 2D J-Res or COSY NMR spectrum and you need to identify peak positions/clusters before downstream analysis such as mixture spectrum simulation or metabolite quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-peak-detection))
- **nmr-spectral-peak-grouping**: Use when raw 1D 1H NMR spectra (spectra matrix + ppm vector) must be summarized into features with minimal information loss, especially when shifts or multiplet structure would make binning/bucketing lossy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-peak-grouping))
- **nmr-spectral-peak-picking**: Use when you have a processed, frequency-domain 2D NMR spectrum (after Fourier transformation, phasing, baseline correction) and need a peak list of cross-peak positions and intensities via local maxima detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-peak-picking))
- **nmr-spectral-phase-and-baseline-preprocessing**: Use when raw 1D 1H NMR spectra of serum/plasma (e.g. Bruker IVDr 600 MHz, MTBLS395-style data) must be ppm-calibrated and baseline-suppressed before metabolite assignment or integration. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-phase-and-baseline-preprocessing))
- **nmr-spectral-phase-correction**: Use when processing a series of 1D-NMR spectra (e.g. metabolomics profiles) whose baselines show phasing/roll, and a manually phase-corrected reference spectrum is available to seed automated ph0/ph1 optimisation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-phase-correction))
- **nmr-spectral-preprocessing**: Use when raw 1H NMR spectra (e.g. EDTA, heparin, or citrate anticoagulated plasma) must be cleaned and formatted into the NMRQNet CSV layout prior to CRNN identification or quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-preprocessing))
- **nmr-spectral-preprocessing-and-phasing**: Use when when working with raw 1H NMR FID data acquired on instruments like Bruker Avance spectrometers that require baseline correction, phase adjustment, and signal alignment before metabolite identification or statistical association testing can proceed reliably. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectral-preprocessing-and-phasing))
- **nmr-spectral-processing**: Use when raw Bruker fid/ser time-domain NMR FID data needs processing into a phased, baseline-corrected spectrum without installing software, or when NUS 2D/pseudo-3D/3D data requires direct-FT or iterative SMILE reconstruction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-processing))
- **nmr-spectral-processing**: Use when you have raw NMR/IR/MS spectral data in vendor-specific (RAW), open (jcamp), or mass spectrometry (mzML) formats and need to parse, validate, and convert them to a standardized internal representation with extracted metadata and peak tables for visualization or further analysis in. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectral-processing))
- **nmr-spectral-realignment**: Use when overlaid/stacked 1D 1H NMR spectra show uncontrolled chemical shift variations between spectra after ppm-scale calibration, i.e. peaks of the same metabolite drift across the spectral dimension. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-realignment))
- **nmr-spectral-recalibration-glucose-anomeric-proton**: Use when raw 1D 1H NMR spectra from Bruker IVDr 600 MHz serum/plasma cohorts show ppm drift that must be corrected before metabolite assignment, bin-based integration, or cross-sample comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-recalibration-glucose-anomeric-proton))
- **nmr-spectral-reconstruction-error-evaluation**: Use when you have raw 1H NMR spectra (csv with chemical shifts in column 1, one column per sample) plus a method's reconstructed spectra and want a per-sample fidelity metric, especially to expose regional reconstruction failure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-reconstruction-error-evaluation))
- **nmr-spectral-reconstruction-mse-optimization**: Use when refining per-metabolite concentration and chemical-shift-position estimates from an initial NMR spectral fitting (e.g., CRNN output) so the reconstructed 1H NMR spectrum minimizes MSE against the original plasma spectrum. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-reconstruction-mse-optimization))
- **nmr-spectral-reproducibility-assessment**: Use when 1H-NMR metabonomic features must be validated for analytical reproducibility via QC-sample CVs, or when deciding whether to filter metabolic features at CV &lt; 0.30 (biomarker discovery) or CV &lt; 0.15 (quantification). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-reproducibility-assessment))
- **nmr-spectral-residual-minimization**: Use when refining per-metabolite concentration and position estimates after an initial deep-learning (CRNN) NMR spectral fit, when reconstruction residuals must be locally minimized via MSE. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-residual-minimization))
- **nmr-spectral-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when you need to verify that a fully automated NMR metabolite-assignment pipeline (SMolESY-select) is fast enough for high-throughput processing of standard 1D 1H NMR serum spectra, e.g. an external cohort like MTBLS395. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-runtime-benchmarking))
- **nmr-spectral-segmental-alignment**: Use when a series of 1D-NMR spectra (e.g. a metabolomics sample set) shows chemical-shift drift within a spectral region and peaks must be aligned to a reference spectrum before multivariate statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-segmental-alignment))
- **nmr-spectral-zero-filling-apodization**: Use when starting from raw Bruker time-domain FID files (fid or ser) and an unprocessed 2D or pseudo-3D NMR spectrum is needed; triggers include 'free induction decay' input and no phased spectrum yet. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectral-zero-filling-apodization))
- **nmr-spectrum-data-import-and-parsing**: Use when you have raw 1D ¹H NMR spectroscopy output consisting of (1) a CSV file with chemical shift and intensity columns and (2) a TXT file listing detected peak chemical shifts, and you need to load and validate these into memory before passing them to a metabolite identification model like. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectrum-data-import-and-parsing))
- **nmr-spectrum-figure-authoring**: Author NMR spectrum-trace teaching figures (1H/13C shifts, first-order splitting/multiplets, integration bands, variable-temperature coalescence) as the editable `nmr_spectrum` ChemTeachingAsset. Use when adding a spectrum figure to a reader chapter, slide deck, or chapter asset manifest — distinct from the proprietary nmr-unique-signals skill (not in this repo; a signal-COUNT reveal, not a spectrum). ([scottmreed/textbook-authoring-skills](https://github.com/scottmreed/textbook-authoring-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/nmr-spectrum-figure-authoring))
- **nmr-spectrum-inference-decoding**: Use when you have a trained multitask spectrum-to-structure checkpoint and simulated 1H/13C NMR spectra (concatenated 28000-point 1H grid plus 80-bin 13C shifts) and need SMILES structure predictions without molecular formula input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectrum-inference-decoding))
- **nmr-spectrum-normalization**: Use when when preparing library compound spectra and observed mixture spectra for mcfNMR analysis, normalize both spectra to cumulative distribution functions (CDFs) scaled by total intensity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectrum-normalization))
- **nmr-spectrum-object-construction**: Use when you have Bruker NMR spectral files (raw instrumental output) and need to prepare them for automated metabolite identification and quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectrum-object-construction))
- **nmr-spectrum-peak-detection**: Use when you have a loaded INADEQUATE NMR spectrum file (after referencing via basic shifting) and need to identify individual peak positions and intensities as input to downstream peak network clustering or metabolite matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectrum-peak-detection))
- **nmr-spectrum-phase-adjustment**: Use when you have acquired raw 1D NMR spectra (FID or processed spectra in NMRPipe format) that exhibit phase distortion—where peaks are not in pure absorption mode—and you need to prepare the spectrum for automatic deconvolution into peak tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectrum-phase-adjustment))
- **nmr-spectrum-phase-and-baseline-correction**: Use when you have Fourier-transformed 1H-NMR spectra (e.g. Protomix spectra_df from 10000-point zero-filled FIDs) that still show phase errors and baseline offsets before referencing, binning, or normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectrum-phase-and-baseline-correction))
- **nmr-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw or semi-processed 1D NMR spectra (¹H and/or ¹³C) from routine laboratory instruments and need to feed them into a CNN–transformer architecture for end-to-end structure elucidation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectrum-preprocessing))
- **nmr-spectrum-probability-measure-representation**: Use when quantifying components of 1H NMR mixture spectra that suffer from overlapping peaks, shifted resonances, low resolution, or distorted lineshapes where peak-based deconvolution methods fail. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectrum-probability-measure-representation))
- **nmr-spectrum-reconstruction-from-library**: Use when you have an NMR spectrum of a mixture sample and a library of reference spectra for individual compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectrum-reconstruction-from-library))
- **nmr-spectrum-simulation**: Use when you need synthetic metabolomic 1H NMR spectra (1D, 2D J-Res, or 2D COSY) of urine, blood, or CSF mixtures — e.g. for benchmarking spectral processing algorithms or reproducing paper comparisons against real spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spectrum-simulation))
- **nmr-spectrum-tiling-format-interpretation**: Use when when loading or creating an NMR spectral dataset (Dataset.createDataFile) and the system must decide between multiple storage backends (SubMatrixFile, BigMappedMatrixFile, MappedSubMatrixFile, MappedMatrixFile). Triggers include: (1) dataset metadata specifies a cache-file flag; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectrum-tiling-format-interpretation))
- **nmr-spectrum-to-structure-inference**: Use when you have 1D NMR spectra (¹H or ¹³C or both) for an unknown organic compound with ≤19 heavy atoms and need to rapidly predict its molecular formula and connectivity graph without manual peak interpretation or exhaustive combinatorial search. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spectrum-to-structure-inference) · 2 copies)
- **nmr-spin-system-feature-construction**: Use when you have 2D NMR (TOCSY + HSQC) peak data from complex natural product mixtures and need discrete, comparable spin system features to drive sample-to-sample similarity networks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spin-system-feature-construction))
- **nmr-spin-system-pattern-search**: Use when you need to assign metabolite signals in calibrated 1D 1H NMR serum/plasma spectra to a curated metabolite panel by matching spin-system patterns inside spectral windows derived from a large cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-spin-system-pattern-search))
- **nmr-spin-system-simulation**: Use when when you have known metabolite concentrations and their spin-system coupling constants (J-values) and need to generate synthetic ¹H NMR spectra for method validation, metabolite library construction, or as a training dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-spin-system-simulation))
- **nmr-star-entry-sequence-extraction**: Use when starting from a BMRB accession (or a local NMR-STAR entry) and you need the protein entity sequence in FASTA format to run BLAST-based cross-linking of BMRB archive data to external public databases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-star-entry-sequence-extraction))
- **nmr-star-file-parsing**: Use when a task requires reading, validating, or extracting saveframes and loops (e.g., the assigned Atom_chem_shift chemical shift loop, NOE/dihedral restraint saveframes) from a BMRB entry in NMR-STAR format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-star-file-parsing))
- **nmr-star-schema-validation**: Use when input is an archival BMRB NMR-STAR entry file that must be parsed, validated against the NMR-STAR 3.2 schema, or converted (e.g., to NEF) before deposition or downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-star-schema-validation))
- **nmr-structure-analysis**: Process, assign, and verify molecular NMR evidence. Use for 1D or 2D solution NMR, FID processing, chemical shifts, multiplicities, couplings, integrations, COSY, HSQC, HMBC, NOE or ROE evidence, mixture assessment, and molecular structure or stereochemical analysis. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/nmr-structure-analysis) · ★4)
- **nmr-structure-annotation**: Use when you have 1H-13C HSQC (optionally multiplicity edited) NMR peak lists from a small natural product or metabolite and need to retrieve candidate structures or nearest known compounds from large chemical databases rather than limited NMR spectral libraries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-structure-annotation))
- **nmr-structure-calculation**: Use when you have NEF files containing NMR constraints for proteins and need to generate an ensemble of ranked structural conformers via torsion-angle dynamics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-structure-calculation))
- **nmr-study-file-validation**: Use when a study downloaded from MetaboLights or Metabolomics Workbench has been converted to CloMet's internal standard (a_ / i_ / m_ / s_ / audit metadata files with per-sample experiment directories holding acqu, acqus, fid) and you must decide whether it is usable downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-study-file-validation))
- **nmr-time-domain-fid-processing**: Use when raw Bruker time-domain FID files (`fid` or `ser`) must be turned into inspectable, phased 2D spectra and no manually tuned processing parameters are available or desired. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-time-domain-fid-processing))
- **nmr-tocsy-hsqc-spin-system-construction**: Use when you have paired TOCSY and HSQC spectra for multiple complex natural products extract/prefraction samples and need shared spin system features to cluster samples or dereplicate known scaffolds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-tocsy-hsqc-spin-system-construction))
- **nmr-voigt-lineshape-fitting**: Use when processed 2D/pseudo-3D NMR spectra contain overlapping cross-peaks requiring per-peak quantitative parameters (amplitudes, widths, relaxation T1/T2, concentrations) that simple peak picking cannot resolve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-voigt-lineshape-fitting))
- **nmr-wine-spectra-multivariate-analysis**: Use when a peak-based NMR feature matrix (e.g. from speaq 2.0 peak picking and grouping) is available and the goal is to test whether samples separate by a known class label such as wine colour via PCA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/nmr-wine-spectra-multivariate-analysis))
- **nmr-workflow-pipeline-execution**: Use when you have raw 1D NMR spectra (FID or processed spectrum files) and need to extract peak parameters (chemical shift, intensity, linewidth) in a tabular format for downstream metabolomic or structural analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmr-workflow-pipeline-execution))
- **nmrbox-environment-management**: Use when when you need to deploy SAND for 1D NMR spectrum deconvolution on a new machine or cluster, or when testing code changes before merging into production. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/nmrbox-environment-management))
- **nmrglue**: Processes calibrated one-dimensional complex NMR free-induction decays with nmrglue into phased spectra, peak candidates, and signed integration regions. Use for raw 1D NMR processing, ppm-axis verification, apodization, Fourier transformation, manual phasing, baseline correction, or reproducible spectral integration. ([KevinZai/commander](https://github.com/KevinZai/commander/tree/HEAD/skills/science-pack/nmrglue) · ★6 · 40 copies)
- **nmrxiv**: Search and download NMR spectroscopy datasets from nmrxiv.org. Use when the user asks to find NMR data, search for HSQC/HMBC/COSY/DEPT experiments, look up compounds by name or SMILES, download NMR datasets, or access nmrxiv.org data programmatically. Outputs JSON for easy parsing. ([shenyimings/skillet](https://github.com/shenyimings/skillet/tree/HEAD/benchmark/wild/dev/safe/steinbeck__nmrxiv-downloader__skills))

- **nms-block-entity**: 實作自定義 NMS BlockEntity（含 NBT serialization、Tick 邏輯、Client sync），比 Bukkit BlockState 更靈活（Paper NMS + Mojang-mapped）/ Implement custom NMS BlockEntity with NBT serialization, tick logic, and client sync ([MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills](https://github.com/MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/nms/nms-block-entity) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **nms-boss-event**: 透過 NMS ServerBossEvent 操作 Boss Bar 進度條、顏色、風格、可見性，實現每人獨立 Boss Bar（Paper NMS + Mojang-mapped）/ Operate Boss Bar progress, color, style, and per-player visibility via NMS ServerBossEvent ([MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills](https://github.com/MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/nms/nms-boss-event) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **nms-build-import**: Copy another player's No Man's Sky base or corvette build into your own save - scan the save for cached builds, extract one, adjust it (rotate/trim/raise), then import it onto a base computer you placed or into a ship, with a hash-verified backup, a rehearsal run, a read-back check and a one-command undo. - **nms-chunk-access**: 透過 NMS LevelChunk 直接讀寫方塊、高度圖、ChunkSection 資料，比 Bukkit Chunk API 更快更底層（Paper NMS + Mojang-mapped）/ Direct LevelChunk block, heightmap, and ChunkSection access for high-performance operations ([MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills](https://github.com/MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/nms/nms-chunk-access) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **nms-custom-entity**: 建立自定義 NMS 實體：繼承現有 Mob 類別、自訂 PathfinderGoal、替換 vanilla 實體行為 / Create custom NMS entities with custom PathfinderGoal AI ([MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills](https://github.com/MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/nms/nms-custom-entity) · ★2 · 4 copies)
- **nms-custom-menu**: 繼承 AbstractContainerMenu 建立自定義容器 GUI，支援 slot 事件攔截與資料同步（Paper NMS + Mojang-mapped）/ Build custom container GUIs by extending AbstractContainerMenu with slot event handling ([MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills](https://github.com/MrPippi/MJP-Paper-Skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/nms/nms-custom-menu) · ★2 · 4 copies)

- **no-fancy-ascii**: Write text using only characters a person types on a normal keyboard. Use this WHENEVER producing or editing human-readable text: website copy, UI strings, headlines, taglines, marketing, READMEs, docs, code comments, commit messages, emails, social posts, or translations (any language). Decorative Unicode (middle dot ·, bullets •, curly quotes “ ” ‘ ’, ellipsis …, arrows → ▸ ▼, non-breaking spaces) reads as machine-generated, because people type straight quotes, three periods, and plain separators. Replace each fancy character with its plain keyboard equivalent; do not swap one ornament for… ([TheArmagan/skills](https://github.com/TheArmagan/skills/tree/HEAD/no-fancy-ascii) · ★3)

- **no-filler-phrases**: Cut empty filler phrases and hype words that make writing read as AI-generated. Use this WHENEVER you produce or edit prose: website copy, UI strings, docs, READMEs, blog posts, emails, social posts, commit messages, or PR descriptions, in any language. Phrases like "in today's fast-paced world", "it's important to note", "when it comes to", "at the end of the day", and hype words like "leverage", "seamless", "robust", "delve", "dive in", "elevate", "unlock", "game-changer" add no information and signal a model wrote the text. - **no-fluff**: Shape output for a busy operator. - **no-fluff-output**: 直给输出法提示语原文。用户抱怨 AI 说话啰嗦绕圈子、想要更直接可执行的回答、看长篇容易走神时使用。触发词包括 别废话、直接说重点、说结论、别绕弯子、有话直说、别来虚的、先说怎么做、拒绝废话、ADHD 友好、专注力不够。整段挂在会话开头，约束之后所有回答的结构，说恢复正常模式才关掉。 ([LearnPrompt/laoyeye](https://github.com/LearnPrompt/laoyeye/tree/HEAD/skills/asking/no-fluff-output) · ★110)
- **no-force-push-a-reviewed-pr**: On a PR that already has reviewers, push follow-up changes as plain new commits only — never `git commit --amend`, never `git push --force` / `--force-with-lease`. Force-push rewrites the commits reviewers' inline comments anchor to, resets the GitHub review state, and forces a cold re-read — the opposite of the incremental trust you are building. Use whenever you push any change to a PR someone has already looked at. Trigger terms: force-push, --amend, --force-with-lease, rebase, "clean up history", pushing to an under-review PR.
